Structural_Audit.md 2026-03-22 06:25:18 
# Structural Audit: OPEN-AIR Encapsulated Modules
**Timestamp:** 2026-03-22
**Auditor:** Gemini (Structural Architect)

## Executive Summary
The overall health of the module encapsulation is **Moderate**. Most `oa*` modules follow the 12-subfolder convention, but there are critical "Gatekeeper" failures and significant "Structural Debt" in the form of hundreds of empty subfolders that should be pruned.

## Critical Violations
Modules that fail the **Root Rule** or lack the **Gatekeeper (Entry.py)**.

| Module | Violation | Details |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `oaComEmber` | **Critical** | Missing `Entry.py`. Root contains `CMakeLists.txt`, `CMakePresets.json`, `config.ini`, `README.md`. |
| `oaDataAudits` | **Critical** | Empty directory. Missing all standard structure and `Entry.py`. |
| `oaDocumentation`| **Minor** | Root violation: `CodeOfConduct.md` should be in `Documentation/` or `Assets/`. |

## Structural Debt
Modules missing core components or housing "Zero-File" zones.

### Missing/Empty Folder Matrix (Sample of Heaviest Debt)
| Module | Missing Standard Folders | Empty Standard Folders (Under-Populated) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `oaComEmber` | None | Almost all (Workers, Interface, Managers, etc.) |
| `oaComAES70` | None | All 12 folders are empty. |
| `oaComBroker` | None | Workers, Interface, Hooks, FileReaders, FileWriters, Assets, Methods, Constants. |
| `oaComSNMP`  | Methods, Tests, Workers, Core, FileWriters | Interface, Documentation, Hooks, FileReaders, Assets, Constants. |
| `oaGuiBuilder`| Workers, Core, Assets | Interface, Managers, Hooks, FileReaders, FileWriters, Tests, Methods, Constants. |

### Zero-File Folders (Cleanup Candidates)
The following folders contain zero files and are candidates for deletion to reduce codebase noise:
- `oaComAES70/Workers`
- `oaComAES70/Interface`
- `oaComAES70/Managers`
- `oaComAES70/Documentation`
- `oaComAES70/Hooks`
- `oaComAES70/FileReaders`
- `oaComAES70/FileWriters`
- `oaComAES70/Assets`
- `oaComAES70/Tests`
- `oaComAES70/Methods`
- `oaComAES70/Constants`
- *(Note: Hundreds of similar empty folders exist across the `oa*` spectrum and should be pruned via a script.)*

## Naming & Architectural Anomalies
- `oaComEmber`: Uses `include/`, `src/`, `subprojects/`, `tests/` (Non-standard C-style layout inside a Python module).
- `oaComVisa`: Contains `Visa_Fleet/` instead of standard names.
- `oaDataSNMP`: Contains `MIB/`, `pass_scripts/` (Domain-specific but non-standard).
- `oaGuiDefinitions`: Contains `left_50/`, `right_50/` (Layout-specific names at root).
- `oaStand_Alone_Utilities`: Contains `Audit Tools/`, `Fluke_Meter/`, `Log_Viewer/`, etc. (Violates encapsulation; these should be modules or assets).

## Refactoring Roadmap
1.  **PRIORITY 1: Gatekeeper Alignment**
    - Create `Entry.py` for `oaComEmber`.
    - Move root files in `oaComEmber` to `Documentation/` or `Assets/`.
2.  **PRIORITY 2: Structural Cleanup**
    - Execute a project-wide prune of empty folders (Zero-File Folders).
3.  **PRIORITY 3: Standard Realignment**
    - Move non-standard folders (e.g., `oaGuiDefinitions/left_50`) into `Assets/` or `Core/`.
    - Standardize `oaComEmber` to follow the Python `oa*` standard rather than its current hybrid layout.

---
*End of Audit Report*
